Result: 在真实交通数据上，完整流程对抗盲人标注的违规达到F1值0.85；在来自两个公开语料库的1,495个合成情景中，监控逻辑在所有情况下均返回预期判定；在两个从官方调查报告重建的历史事故中，监控器识别出与调查人员记录的相同的程序偏差。

Conclusion: 该运行时验证框架能有效监控管制员与飞行员之间的程序性交互，在实际交通中具有较高检测准确率（F1=0.85），在合成场景中完全正确，并能从历史事故中重现调查人员识别的程序偏差，验证了框架的实用性和可靠性。

Abstract: Air traffic control procedures are executed through spoken exchanges between controllers and pilots. These interactions are essential to the safety of air transportation: failures in their execution can create severe operational hazards, as evidenced by past fatal accidents. Assessing whether an instruction has been followed requires relating what was said to the aircraft concerned, its state, and the obligations that pilots must meet. We present a runtime verification framework that monitors such procedures by checking controller-pilot exchanges, surveillance data, and onboard observations. The framework parses radio communications into events linked to the entities they concern and merges them with surveillance and onboard observations into a time-stamped trace. The ICAO-derived obligations as formalized as temporal formulas with explicit time bounds and evaluated over execution traces. Every violation is reported along with the breached obligations and the observations that support the verdict. With real traffic, the complete pipeline reaches an F1 of 0.85 against blind human-annotated violations; in 1,495 synthetic situations derived from two public corpora, the monitor logic returns the expected verdict in every case. In two historical accidents reconstructed from official investigation reports, the monitor identifies the same procedural deviations documented by the investigators.

</details>


### [205] [How Robust Are Automated Fact-Checking Systems? A Cross-Benchmark Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25934)
*Aida Usmanova,Zangir Iklassov,Markus Leippold,Ricardo Usbeck*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对九种自动化事实核查（AFC）模型在四个跨领域数据集（科学、开放网络、气候）上进行了两阶段检索-验证全流程的基准测试，发现：仅凭声称的微调模型优于零样本LLM和AVeriTeC 2025顶级系统；系统排名高度依赖领域和评估指标；检索仍是AFC的主要瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AFC评估忽略了简单基线，系统仅在单一基准上开发，无法保证跨领域泛化。此前没有工作对完整的检索-验证两阶段流程进行跨数据集交叉评估。

Method: 对九个模型进行基准测试，涵盖随机基线、稀疏检索模型、微调Transformer、零样本LLM以及AVeriTeC 2025共享任务中排名前二的系统，在科学、开放网络和气候四个数据集上评估完整的两阶段检索-验证管线。

Result: 三大发现：(1)在ClimateCheck上，仅用声称的微调模型胜过零样本LLM和顶级AVeriTeC 2025系统，噪声证据会降低真实性预测；(2)系统排名强依赖于领域和指标，最佳模型在SciFact上macro-F1为0.70但在ClimateCheck上仅0.31；(3)用金标注替换检索证据可使真实性准确率提升14-22个点，确认检索是主要瓶颈。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了AFC系统跨领域泛化不足、检索环节是核心瓶颈等问题，并公开了代码、预处理数据集和所有结果以支持可复现研究。

Abstract: Automated fact-checking (AFC) systems retrieve evidence and predict claim veracity, yet evaluations omit simple baselines, systems are developed for a single benchmark and cannot be trusted to generalise across domains. No prior work cross-evaluates the full two-stage retrieve-then-verify pipeline across diverse datasets, complementing retrieval-only studies (Thakur et al., 2021) and single-stage benchmarking studies (Calamai et al., 2025). We benchmark nine models, ranging from random and sparse baselines to fine-tuned transformers, zero-shot LLMs, and the two highest-ranked systems from the AVeriTeC 2025 shared task, across four datasets spanning scientific, open-web, and climate domains. Three findings stand out: (1) on ClimateCheck claim-only and fine-tuned models outperform zero-shot LLM and top-performing AVeriTeC 2025 systems, highlighting that noisy evidence can degrade veracity prediction; (2) system rankings are strongly domain- and metric-dependent: the best model on SciFact (macro-F1 0.70) drops to 0.31 on ClimateCheck, while the AVeriTeC 2025 winner and runner-up swap rankings based on evaluation metrics and datasets; (3) replacing retrieved evidence with gold annotations improves veracity accuracy by 14-22 points across models, confirming retrieval remains primary bottleneck. We release code, pre-processed datasets, and all results to support reproducible AFC research.

</details>


### [206] [LivingRAG: Augmenting Graph RAG with Experience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25960)
*Yuzhuo Cui,Zongye Zhang,Qingjie Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LivingRAG是一种可写可复用的图RAG框架，通过存储和重用已验证的推理经验（图信号和摘要），提升多跳问答的准确率并减少推理token消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图RAG系统每次查询独立处理，丢弃LLM推理后的有用经验，导致后续相关查询需要从头检索和推理，效率低下且浪费计算资源。

Method: LivingRAG在基于图的检索骨干上增加一个可写的经验存储库，通过两种方式复用已验证经验：1）存储的图信号帮助检索定位先前相关查询中有用的实体和段落；2）存储的摘要为答案生成提供参考推理模式。同时分析在线QA流，从共享实体、图邻域和问题模板中提取可重用信号。

Result: 在多跳QA基准测试中，LivingRAG相比强RAG基线提升了准确率，并且在重用相关先前经验时减少了完成token的使用量。

Conclusion: 通过在RAG系统中引入可写可复用的经验存储机制，能够有效利用历史推理结果提升后续查询的性能和效率，证明了跨查询知识重用的价值。

Abstract: Graph-based RAG improves multi-hop question answering by organizing evidence as a knowledge graph. However, most existing RAG systems process each query in isolation and discard useful reasoning from the LLM's response after inference. As a result, later related queries need to retrieve evidence and reason from scratch. We propose LivingRAG, a Graph RAG framework with writable and reusable reasoning experience. LivingRAG adds a writable experience store to a graph-based retrieval backbone, enabling verified experiences to be reused during inference in two ways. Stored graph signals help retrieval find entities and passages that were useful in earlier related queries. Stored summaries provide a reference reasoning pattern for answer generation. We analyze online QA streams and find reusable signals from shared entities, graph neighborhoods, and question templates. Experiments on multi-hop QA benchmarks show that LivingRAG improves accuracy over strong RAG baselines and reduces completion-token use when relevant prior experience is reused.

</details>


### [207] [Quantitative Analysis of $ω$-Regular Robust MDPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25968)
*Ali Asadi,Krishnendu Chatterjee,Ehsan Kafshdar Goharshady,Mehrdad Karrabi,Alipasha Montaseri,Ali Shafiee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有线性定义不确定性集合的(s,a)-矩形鲁棒马尔可夫决策过程(RMDP)的定量奇偶性目标问题，证明了代理和环境都存在纯记忆最优策略，并提出了一种多项式时间算法结合策略迭代求解定量奇偶性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前工作仅研究了RMDP的定性分析（几乎必然/正概率满足问题），而精确的定量分析（即最坏情况下的最大满足概率）尚未解决，这是从定性到定量的自然推进。

Method: 首先证明代理和环境均存在纯记忆最优策略；其次为线性定义的鲁棒马尔可夫链设计多项式时间算法，并将其作为子程序用于RMDP的策略迭代算法；该算法结合了定量单步改进与定性几乎必然改进。

Result: 提出了求解定量奇偶性问题的策略迭代算法，并报告了与显式归约为随机博弈方法相比的实验结果。

Conclusion: 本文解决了线性定义不确定性集合下RMDP定量奇偶性问题的精确求解，填补了先前定性分析之外的空白，并为实践提供了有效的算法。

Abstract: Robust Markov Decision Processes (RMDPs) generalize classical MDPs by allowing uncertainty in transition probabilities and optimizing against their worst-case realization. We consider $(s,a)$-rectangular RMDPs with \emph{linearly defined} uncertainty sets and study parity objectives, which are a canonical representation of $ω$-regular objectives. An uncertainty set is linearly defined if it is described by linear inequalities over the transition distribution together with auxiliary variables, which capture the standard $L_1$ and $L_\infty$ balls as well as general polytopic uncertainty sets.
  The quantitative value is the supremum, over all agent policies, of the satisfaction probability guaranteed against the adversarial environment. Previous work studied the qualitative analysis, namely the almost-sure (resp. positive) problem that asks whether a single agent policy guarantees satisfaction with probability one (resp. positive probability) against every environment policy. In this work, we solve the exact quantitative problem.
  Our contributions are threefold. First, we show that both the agent and the environment admit pure memoryless optimal policies. Second, we give a polynomial-time algorithm for quantitative parity on linearly defined robust Markov chains and use it as a subroutine in a policy-iteration algorithm for RMDPs. The algorithm combines quantitative one-step improvements with qualitative almost-sure improvements. Finally, we report experiments comparing our approach with the explicit reduction to stochastic games.

</details>


### [208] [SciMIF: Understanding Multimodal Instruction Following in Scientific Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25973)
*Ye Shen,Yuting Zheng,Dun Pei,Zijian Chen,Wenlong Zhang,Qi Jia,Guangtao Zhai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SciMIF基准，用于评估多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在科学领域遵循复杂指令的能力。基于22个任务、5个学科的分析，构建了包含10个约束组的分类体系，并通过高保真指令注入管道增强现有科学数据集。实验发现当前MLLMs在不同学科间表现差异显著（化学最具挑战性），且模型规模增大并不带来约束遵循能力的相应提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学领域中对指令遵循能力的评估存在空白，现有MLLMs在科学应用中的多模态指令遵循能力尚未被系统性地量化评估，需要建立专门的基准来推动科学领域的模型发展。

Method: 1) 分析22个任务和5个代表性科学学科，构建包含10个约束组的分类体系；2) 基于该分类体系开发高保真指令注入管道，系统性地增强现有科学数据集；3) 在多个先进的开源和闭源MLLMs上进行综合实验评估。

Result: 实验显示不同科学学科间存在显著的性能差异，化学对当前MLLMs更具挑战性；模型规模增大并未带来约束遵循能力的相应提升；当前模型在细粒度约束以及需要深度应用学科知识的指令上仍表现困难。

Conclusion: SciMIF填补了多模态指令遵循评估在科学领域的空白，为未来在严谨科学应用中改进MLLMs奠定了重要基础。数据和代码将公开发布。

Abstract: Understanding instruction-following capabilities in scientific domains is essential for effectively leveraging Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) to advance the development of scientific fields. In this work, we introduce SciMIF, a novel benchmark designed to evaluate the capability of MLLMs in following complex scientific instructions. Specifically, based on an extensive analysis of 22 distinct tasks across 5 representative scientific disciplines, we propose a comprehensive taxonomy comprising 10 constraint groups that captures both general functional requirements and discipline-specific characteristics. Guided by this taxonomy, we develop a high-fidelity instruction injection pipeline to systematically augment existing scientific datasets. We conduct comprehensive experiments on multiple state-of-the-art closed-source and open-source MLLMs. Our findings reveal significant performance disparities across different scientific disciplines, with chemistry posing greater challenges for current MLLMs. Furthermore, we observe that increasing the model scale does not yield corresponding improvements in constraint adherence, and current models still struggle severely with fine-grained constraints and instructions requiring the deep application of disciplinary knowledge. SciMIF fills the current void in evaluating multimodal instruction adherence within scientific domains, laying a crucial foundation for future enhancements of MLLMs in rigorous scientific applications. Data and code will be released at https://github.com/shenye7436/SciMIF .

</details>


### [209] [Multi-Granularity Context-Enhanced RAG over Multimodal Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25986)
*Zongyu Wu,Yilong Wang,Xiaochen Wang,Minhua Lin,Zhichao Xu,Fenglong Ma,Xiang Zhang,Suhang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CEMMKG框架，通过为知识图谱中的每个图像补充局部和全局范围的文本上下文（局部上下文包含语义相关的句子，全局上下文提供段落摘要），并引入多粒度设计，以缩小图文语义差距，从而提升多模态知识图谱增强的检索增强生成（GraphRAG）性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于多模态知识图谱（MMKG）的RAG方法通常在不同模态独立处理后再融合，导致视觉信息提取和后续多模态融合时对文本上下文的利用非常有限，造成图像与文本之间的语义鸿沟，限制了多模态GraphRAG的性能。

Method: 提出一种构建上下文增强多模态知识图谱（CEMMKG）的新框架。该框架为每个图像在局部和全局两个范围内补充文本上下文：局部上下文在周围文本基础上引入与图像语义相关的句子，并采用多粒度设计以捕捉不同细节层级的语义相关信息；全局上下文则提供整个段落的摘要。

Result: 在视觉中心的数据集上进行的大量实验验证了CEMMKG在利用上下文信息提升基于MMKG的RAG性能方面的有效性，且在不同MMKG-based RAG方法上的实验结果表明其具有广泛的适用性。

Conclusion: CEMMKG通过上下文增强的多模态知识图谱有效弥合了图像与文本之间的语义鸿沟，显著提升了多模态GraphRAG的性能，并且作为一种通用框架，可以适用于不同的MMKG-based RAG方法。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) is widely used to mitigate hallucination issues in large language models (LLMs) and multimodal large language models (MLLMs). In particular, knowledge graph (KG)-based RAG leverages structured knowledge to provide (M)LLMs with high-quality external information. Building on these works, recent studies have explored multimodal knowledge graphs (MMKGs) as knowledge bases for GraphRAG. This enables Graph RAG to integrate knowledge across multiple modalities, thereby further enhancing its performance. However, existing MMKG-based RAG methods generally follow a common pipeline in which different modalities are largely processed independently before being fusion. As a result, textual context is only used to a limited extent during visual information extraction and subsequent multimodal knowledge fusion. This brings a semantic gap between images and text which limits the multimodal GraphRAG performance. To address this issue, we propose a novel framework for constructing a Context-Enhanced MMKG (CEMMKG) to better support multimodal GraphRAG. The proposed CEMMKG enriches each image with complementary textual context at both local and global scopes. Local context goes beyond the surrounding text by incorporating sentences that are semantically related to the image, while global context provides a summary of the entire passage. We further introduce a multi-granularity design for the local context, allowing it to capture semantically relevant information at different levels of detail. Extensive experiments on the selected vision-centric dataset validate that CEMMKG is effective in leveraging contextual information to improve MMKG-based RAG performance. Moreover, its effectiveness across different MMKG-based RAG methods demonstrates its broad applicability.

</details>


### [210] [AsymSpec: Context-Asymmetric Speculative Decoding for Agentic LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26004)
*Sheng Liang,Yongyue Zhang,Nathanael Brian,Hang Lv,Hao Wang,Chen Zhang,Yong Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AsymSpec，一个非对称投机解码框架：轻量级草稿模型读取完整上下文，而大型验证模型在压缩视图上运行，通过对比性δ-logits融合和发散感知接受门控，在保持约90%全上下文准确率的同时实现1.3-1.7倍吞吐量提升和0.2-0.3倍计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agentic LLM流水线因上下文累积导致推理成本不断上升，传统的输入压缩虽然能控制延迟但会降低任务准确率；而投机解码（SD）虽然能无损加速生成，但假设草稿模型和验证模型共享相同上下文，无法解决准确率-开销之间的权衡问题。

Method: AsymSpec打破SD的对称性假设：轻量级草稿模型读取完整输入，大型验证模型处理压缩视图。草稿模型通过对比性δ-logits融合来引导验证模型，并由发散感知接受门控调节，以维持验证稳定性和高草稿接受率。

Result: 在四个agentic能力和两个端到端agent基准上，AsymSpec平均达到约90%的全上下文准确率，在孤立文本能力上实现1.3-1.7倍的吞吐量加速，计算成本仅为原来的0.2-0.3倍。

Conclusion: 非对称上下文访问在压缩丢弃关键推理信号时能产生显著收益，表明该方法有效地解决了准确率-开销权衡问题。

Abstract: Agentic LLM pipelines face escalating inference costs as context accumulates across retrieval, tool use, and multi-turn interactions. To control latency, deployments routinely compress inputs, but this degrades task accuracy. Speculative decoding (SD) accelerates generation losslessly, yet it assumes the drafter and verifier share an identical context, preventing SD from resolving the accuracy-overhead trade-off. We propose AsymSpec, an asymmetric speculative decoding framework that breaks this symmetry: a lightweight drafter reads the full input while the large verifier operates on the compressed view. The drafter steers the verifier via a contrastive $δ$-fusion of logits, modulated by a divergence-aware acceptance gate that preserves verification stability and high draft acceptance rates. Evaluated across four agentic capabilities and two end-to-end agent benchmarks, AsymSpec reaches $\approx 90\%$ of full-context accuracy on average, delivering $1.3$--$1.7\times$ throughput speedups at $0.2$--$0.3\times$ the compute cost on isolated text capabilities. These results show that asymmetric context access yields substantial gains precisely when compression discards critical reasoning signals.

</details>


### [211] [Imitation Learning for Connection-Tableau Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26009)
*Fredrik Rømming,Mantas Bakšys,Martin S. Fixman,Sean B. Holden*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将自动定理证明的逐步构造过程建模为形式演算诱导的转换系统上的策略，使用图神经网络对证明编辑进行评分，通过模仿学习训练策略，并在多个基准上展示了比leanCoP多解46%的问题且步数少一个数量级的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动定理证明器在每一步构建证明时需要选择添加和删除什么内容，现有方法（如leanCoP风格搜索和plCoP/rlCoP风格规划）缺乏统一的策略学习接口，难以直接应用策略学习方法来改进证明搜索效率。

Method: 将证明构建过程形式化为由形式演算（基于子句连接表）诱导的转换系统上的策略，统一了leanCoP风格搜索和plCoP/rlCoP风格规划；策略配备图神经网络，跨问题转移结构信息来对证明编辑进行评分；通过从发现的证明中模仿学习训练策略，并逐步移除搜索脚手架进行评测。

Result: 在M2k、MPTP2078-bushy和TPTP v9.2.1基准上，固定步数预算内，学习到的策略比leanCoP多解决46%的问题，并且达到证明所需的步数少一个数量级。

Conclusion: 统一的策略接口结合图神经网络和模仿学习，能够有效提升自动定理证明性能，即使在移除符号回溯等搜索支撑后，纯策略驱动的推理仍具竞争力。

Abstract: An automated theorem prover builds a proof step by step, choosing at each point what to add and what to remove. We cast this construction as a policy acting in a transition system induced by a formal calculus, which fixes which steps are sound: for clausal connection tableaux, leanCoP-style search and plCoP/rlCoP-style planning then become stateful policies over one interface, and policy-learning methods apply directly. We equip such policies with a graph neural network that scores proof edits from structure that transfers across problems, train it by imitation learning from found proofs, and measure how performance holds as we remove search scaffolding, from full symbolic backtracking to a policy the network drives alone. Within a fixed step budget on M2k, MPTP2078-bushy, and TPTP v9.2.1, learned policies solve up to 46% more problems than leanCoP, and reach proofs in an order of magnitude fewer steps.

</details>


### [212] [Trace Integrity for LLM Data Agents: A Vision for Auditable Structured Reasoning in Real-World Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26036)
*Srimonti Dutta,Akshata Kishore Moharir*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出仅用答案准确率评估LLM数据代理是不够的，因为基准测试中的正确答案可能来自无效的推理轨迹。作者提出"轨迹完整性"（Trace Integrity）作为部署可靠性标准，并引入执行契约（Execution Contracts）和CAIT率（正确答案/无效轨迹率）来弥补答案导向评估的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的答案准确率评估在结构化数据任务中不可靠——基准测试中的正确答案可能是由无效轨迹生成的。自然语言推理和自由格式的理由无法可靠地指定真实系统所需的操作级程序，存在"结构缺口"（Structure Gap）这一部署失效模式，因此需要新的可靠性评估标准。

Method: 提出轨迹完整性（Trace Integrity）标准，包含显式性、可执行性、模式有效性、算子忠实性、可重放性、答案一致性和可审计性七个维度。通过执行契约（Execution Contracts）将这些标准操作化，契约将用户意图与模式元素、算子计划、假设、可执行查询、验证状态和最终答案关联绑定。同时提出CAIT率用于度量答案导向评估将计算上无支持的输出视为成功的频率。

Result: 在BIRD Mini-Dev数据集上，Direct SQL、Operation Summary + SQL和Contract-First SQL的答案准确率分别为20%、22%和24%，但它们的轨迹完整性通过率分别为39%、43%和40%，CAIT率分别为55%、59.1%和45.8%，证明答案准确率、轨迹有效性和静默失败风险是三个不同的评估信号。

Conclusion: 真实世界的LLM数据代理不仅应评估其输出是否匹配参考答案，还应评估其输出是否有可审计的计算过程支撑。答案准确率、轨迹有效性和静默失败风险是三个相互独立的评估维度，不能混为一谈。

Abstract: Answer accuracy is an insufficient reliability signal for LLM data agents. In structured-data tasks, a benchmark-correct answer can be produced by an invalid trace. This paper introduces Trace Integrity, a deployment reliability criterion for evaluating whether the computation recorded behind an answer is explicit, executable, schema-valid, operator-faithful, replayable, answer-consistent, and auditable. We identify the Structure Gap as the deployment failure mode that makes Trace Integrity necessary: natural-language reasoning and free-form rationales do not reliably specify the operator-level programs required by real-world systems. We operationalize Trace Integrity with execution contracts, structured artifacts that bind user intent to schema elements, operator plans, assumptions, executable queries, verification status, and final-answer linkage. We also introduce CAIT (Correct Answer / Invalid Trace) Rate, which measures how often answer-only evaluation counts computationally unsupported outputs as successes. In an empirical demonstration on BIRD Mini-Dev, Direct SQL, Operation Summary + SQL, and Contract-First SQL achieve answer accuracies of 20%, 22%, and 24%, while their Trace Integrity Pass Rates are 39%, 43%, and 40% and their CAIT Rates remain high at 55%, 59.1%, and 45.8%, showing that answer accuracy, trace validity, and silent-failure risk are distinct evaluation signals. Real-world LLM data agents should, therefore, be evaluated not only by whether their outputs match a reference answer, but by whether those outputs are backed by auditable computation.

</details>


### [213] [SwarmWorld: Stigmergic technological evolution in societies of language-model agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26081)
*Subhadeep Pal,Fiona Y. Wang,Markus J. Buehler*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了去中心化LLM智能体能否在共享环境中自我组织并构建功能性技术社会。通过SwarmWorld框架，初始同质的LLM智能体无需预定义角色即可自主分化出探索、建设、维护和协调等行为，形成比独立搜索基线更广泛且更具韧性的技术组合，但最强单一技术的产出上独立搜索仍具竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大部分多智能体系统依赖直接对话、预定义角色或集中式工作流，尚不清楚去中心化智能体能否自主组织并构建功能性技术。作者希望探索语言模型智能体作为集体智能涌现的新底物，验证其在无人工预设下能否演化出技术社会。

Method: 提出SwarmWorld框架：智能体在空间环境中探索、处理资源、测试材料并构建持久化工件，编写可执行控制器，由确定性模拟器在智能体被移除后施加未知扰动进行评估。该框架将认知与后果分离——智能体在固定模式和材料规则下提出架构，由模拟世界决定功能。作者比较了共享社会与best-of-N独立搜索基线，并测试了显式文化机制与物理stigmergy的作用。

Result: 共享社会产生比强best-of-N隔离搜索基线更广泛、更具韧性的技术组合，但隔离搜索在最强调艺品上仍具竞争力。智能体自发分化为探索、建设、维护和协调行为，并随世界成熟而转型。技术通过协作建设、可执行继承和持久智能体-工件网络不断累积，多数重用始于物理观察而非交流。显式文化机制虽能放大协作，但功能收益取决于结果和时标。仅靠物理stigmergy即可支持有能力的社会。

Conclusion: 物理stigmergy单独足以支撑有能力的去中心化智能体社会，交互驱动的是持久技术生态的形成而非普遍更优的个体发明。去中心化LLM智能体通过共享环境能够实现集体智能的涌现和技术文明的演化，但需要在"技术多样性"和"个体最优性"之间作出权衡。

Abstract: Collective intelligence can emerge when individuals coordinate through a shared environment, allowing local actions to accumulate into durable social organization. Language-model agents offer a new substrate for this process, yet most multi-agent systems rely on direct conversation, predefined roles, or centralized workflows. It remains unclear whether decentralized agents can build functional technologies and outperform independent search. Here, initially homogeneous LLM agents in SwarmWorld self-organize without assigned roles or recipes into evolving technological societies. Agents explore a spatial environment, process resources, test materials, construct persistent artifacts, and write executable controllers evaluated by a deterministic simulator under unseen disturbances after the agents are removed. SwarmWorld splits cognition from consequence: agents propose architectures and controllers within fixed action and material schemas, while the simulated world determines function. Shared societies develop broader, more resilient technological portfolios than a strong best-of-N isolated-search baseline, although isolated search remains competitive for the strongest artifact. Agents differentiate into exploration, construction, maintenance, and coordination behaviors, transitioning as the world matures. Technologies accumulate through collaborative construction, executable inheritance, and persistent agent-artifact networks, with most reuse beginning through physical observation rather than communication. Explicit cultural mechanisms amplify collaboration and organization, but functional benefits depend on outcome and timescale. Physical stigmergy alone supports capable societies, while interaction drives persistent technological ecologies rather than universally superior individual inventions.

</details>


### [214] [Planetary Prediction Engine: Autonomous Geospatial Prediction via Intelligent Data Selection and Foundation Model Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26088)
*Evelyn Ma,Rama Kumar Pasumarthi,Kishwar Shafin,Mandar Sharma,Mimi Sun,Hamed Sadeghi,Dav M. Ebengo,Mbulayi Onesime,Rouslan Solomakhin,John Wamburu,William Ogallo,Aisha Walcott-Bryant,Sanxing Chen,Arbaaz Muslim,Yael Mayer,Ronald Ho,Roy Lee,Ruth Alcantara,Abdoulaye Diack,Monica Bharel,Lambert Rosique,Jeremy Amez-Droz,Christopher Haire,James Manyika,Yossi Matias,Niv Efron,Gautam Prasad,Shravya Shetty*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出行星预测引擎（PPE），一个端到端的自主AI系统，能够直接从自然语言查询出发，自动完成多模态数据检索、融合与模型选择，在地理空间预测任务中显著超越现有最先进方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建预测性行星模型面临数据生态碎片化的瓶颈，传统方法需要人工完成数据检索、多模态数据整理融合以及迭代模型选择，效率低下且技术门槛高。

Method: PPE接收自然语言查询，自动从开放网络和地球观测平台（Data Commons、Google Earth Engine）检索时空相关协变量，并将这些数据与地理空间基础模型嵌入（PDFM、AlphaEarth）融合，同时自动搜索任务定制化的模型架构族并配备自动化过拟合防护机制。

Result: PPE在多个任务中大幅超越基线：美国21个CDC健康指标的R²从60.0%提升至76.8%，FEMA国家风险指数从60.0%提升至64.9%，社会脆弱性指数从58.6%提升至66.2%；尼日利亚粮食安全指标R²从31.5%翻倍至66.1%；2026年刚果（金）埃博拉疫情nowcasting的Recall@10达到83.3%，较公开最先进方法提升10.3个百分点。

Conclusion: PPE通过将自主多模态行星数据发现与定向模型优化相结合，大幅降低了行星尺度分析的技术门槛，能够实现快速、定制化、专家级别的部署。

Abstract: Addressing critical global challenges, from food security and disaster risk to disease outbreaks and socio-economic vulnerability, demands high-fidelity geospatial modeling. However, building predictive planetary models remains bottlenecked by a fragmented data ecosystem, requiring manual data retrieval, multimodal data curation and fusion along with iterative model selection. We present the Planetary Prediction Engine (PPE), an autonomous AI system that executes this end-to-end workflow directly from natural-language queries. PPE synthesizes multimodal datasets on the fly, retrieving spatiotemporally relevant covariates across open-web and Earth observation platforms (Data Commons, Google Earth Engine) and fusing them with geospatial foundation model embeddings (PDFM, AlphaEarth). Simultaneously, it searches over task-tailored model architecture families with automated overfitting guards. Across diverse tasks, geographies, and scientific domains, PPE consistently outperforms state-of-the-art or manually tuned expert baselines. For US spatial regression, PPE improves mean $R^2$ across 21 CDC health indicators (76.8% vs. 60.0%), FEMA national risk indices (64.9% vs. 60.0%), and the Social Vulnerability Index (66.2% vs. 58.6%). For spatial downscaling in data-scarce settings, PPE integrates localized proxies to double baseline accuracy in Nigerian food security indicators ($R^2$ of 66.1% vs. 31.5%). For epidemiological nowcasting of the 2026 DRC Bundibugyo Ebola outbreak, PPE achieves a Recall@10 of 83.3% (identifying 15 of 18 newly invaded health zones across five weekly forecasts), a +10.3 percentage-point improvement over the public state-of-the-art modeling (~73%). By combining autonomous multimodal planetary data discovery with targeted model optimization, PPE lowers the technical barrier to planetary-scale analytics, enabling rapid, customized, expert-level deployment.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [215] [Secret MCP: Evidence-Bounded and Context-Isolated Design Specification Generation from Web Screenshots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24944)
*Yeongjin Jo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文介绍Secret MCP，一个开源本地系统，为每个公共Web参考生成单一可审计的设计规范。它通过分离检索、证据准备、模型调用、存储和检查，将截图处理为带重叠的缩放切片，并通过19节合同标注测量、观察、推断和未知的声明，避免多参考提示中的证据污染问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有截图转代码系统仅优化渲染实现，但截图缺少文档结构、交互逻辑、响应式规则和来源信息，难以区分观测与猜测；多参考提示还会导致一个参考的证据或推断污染另一个参考，缺乏可审计性。

Method: Secret MCP将流程分为检索、证据准备、模型调用、存储和检查五部分。长截图被缩放并带重叠地切片；证据记录保留准备空间和源空间坐标及测量调色板。采用19节合同覆盖页面清单、导航几何、响应式矩阵、组件、可访问性、验收标准等，并显式标注measured/observed/inferred/unknown声明。参考逐一通过采样器接口顺序处理，MCP适配器设置includeContext为none。

Result: 在commit c130c9c上进行了两级评估：实时检索与夹具模型集成测试选择了两个参考（排除第三个）、准备了九张证据图像、发出两次采样请求，生成两份零交叉引用标识符的文档；对三个外部生成的设计索引进行静态审计，所有文档包含全部19个必需章节，每份文档一个唯一参考标识符，共8个页面规格、1,943个像素值测量和319个颜色字面量。测试确立的是编排不变量和语法合同合规性，而非语义或视觉重建准确性。

Conclusion: 采样器抽象在直接模型API和传输之间保持了这些边界，尽管MCP采样将于2026年弃用，该设计仍确保了系统的可审计性和隔离性。

Abstract: Screenshot-to-code systems optimize for rendered implementations, but screenshots omit document structure, interaction logic, responsive rules, and provenance needed to distinguish observation from guesswork. Multi-reference prompts also risk contaminating one reference with evidence or inferences from another. We present Secret MCP, an open-source local system that produces one auditable design specification per public web reference. It separates retrieval, evidence preparation, model invocation, storage, and inspection. Long captures are resized and tiled with overlap; evidence records preserve prepared- and source-space coordinates and a measured color palette. A 19-section contract covers page inventories, navigation geometry, responsive matrices, components, accessibility, acceptance criteria, and explicit labels for measured, observed, inferred, and unknown claims. References are processed sequentially through a sampler interface. The evaluated MCP adapter sends one sampling/createMessage request per reference with includeContext set to none; a fresh-process adapter provides a stronger boundary.
  We evaluate commit c130c9c at two levels. A live retrieval and fixture-model integration run selected two references after excluding a third, prepared nine evidence images, issued two sampling requests, and produced two documents with zero cross-reference identifier occurrences. A static audit of three externally generated design indexes found all 19 required sections in every document, one unique reference identifier per document, eight page specifications, 1,943 pixel-valued measurements, and 319 color literals. These tests establish orchestration invariants and syntactic contract compliance, not semantic or visual reconstruction accuracy. The sampler abstraction preserves these boundaries across direct model APIs and transports despite MCP sampling's 2026 deprecation.

</details>


### [216] [The Evolution of Binary Decompilation in the Modern Era: A Taxonomy, Literature Review, and Future Perspectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24955)
*Omar Abusabha,Sungjae Hwang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对过去几十年的反编译研究进行了系统性综述，构建了当代研究方法论的全面分类体系，并分析了评估指标、工具和基准测试的趋势，指出了地面真值缺乏和标准化基准缺失等关键挑战，最后展望了未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反编译已成为软件工程和安全分析中的基础技术，正在与现代机器学习方法融合。然而，该领域缺乏系统性的综述来梳理研究进展、方法论分类和评估标准，研究者难以把握整体研究格局和比较不同方法的优劣。

Method: 对过去数十年发表的反编译研究进行系统性文献回顾，开发了涵盖当代研究方法论的全面分类体系（taxonomy），并分析了评估指标、工具和基准测试的使用趋势。

Result: 揭示了该领域面临的关键挑战，包括缺乏可靠的地面真值（ground truth）以及标准化基准测试的缺失，这些问题阻碍了不同方法之间的严格比较。

Conclusion: 研究指出了反编译领域的核心瓶颈，并提出了未来的研究方向，呼吁建立更可靠的评估体系和标准化基准以推动该领域的进一步发展。

Abstract: Decompilation has become a foundational technique in software engineering and security analysis, and it is now advancing through the integration of modern machine learning (ML) approaches. This article presents a systematic review of decompilation studies published over the past decades and develops a comprehensive taxonomy of methodologies employed in contemporary research. We further examine trends in evaluation metrics, tools, and benchmarks used to assess state-of-the-art approaches. Our review reveals key challenges, such as the lack of reliable ground truth and the absence of standardized benchmarks, which hinder rigorous comparison. Finally, we outline future research directions.

</details>


### [217] [Evaluating and Preventing Security Smells in AI-Generated Ansible Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24962)
*Pandu Ranga Reddy Konala,Vimal Kumar,David Bainbridge,Junaid Haseeb*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文首次评估了16种AI模型生成的Ansible基础设施即代码（IaC）是否符合安全要求。研究发现，在无安全指导的情况下，所有16个模型生成的代码均包含安全异味（security smells），无法满足CIS基准合规。作者提出基于扩展CO-STAR框架的安全提示方法，将Ansible最佳实践和CIS基准融入提示词，使4/16的模型能够生成合规代码，最佳模型的CIS合规率达到95%-100%，比人类开发者（23%-43%）提升四倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI编码助手越来越多地被用于生成基础设施即代码，但此前没有任何研究检验这些代码是否满足安全要求。由于基础设施代码中的安全异味会传播到部署的系统，产生不安全且不可信的底层架构，这一问题亟待解决。

Method: 评估16种AI模型生成Apache Tomcat v10和MongoDB v7的Ansible角色，对278个Ansible角色进行CIS基准分析。提出一种将Ansible最佳实践和CIS基准融入提示词的扩展CO-STAR框架，实现安全异味在综合阶段而非部署后被预防。

Result: 无安全指导时所有16个模型生成的代码均含安全异味。应用新方法后，4/16的模型能生成合规代码，领先模型达到95%-100%的CIS合规率，相比人类开发者（23%-43%）提升四倍，整体代码质量提升19%-49%。其余12个模型失败的原因是缺乏多约束指令遵循能力，而非代码生成能力不足。

Conclusion: 对于能力足够的模型，该方法无需重新训练，可通过系统提示词直接采用，实现从"部署后检测"向"合成时预防"的范式转变，有效提升AI生成基础设施代码的安全性。

Abstract: AI coding assistants generate Infrastructure as Code, yet no work has examined whether this code meets security requirements. This matters because security smells in infrastructure code propagate to deployed systems, producing infrastructure that is insecure and untrustworthy. We evaluate 16 AI models generating Ansible roles for Apache Tomcat v10 and MongoDB v7, analysing 278 Ansible roles against CIS benchmarks. Without security guidance, all 16 AI models produced code containing security smells, resulting in vulnerable infrastructure that fails compliance verification and underperforms code written by human developers. We introduce an approach integrating Ansible best practices and CIS benchmarks into prompts through an extended CO-STAR framework, enabling security smell prevention during synthesis rather than detection after deployment. When this approach is applied, 4 out of 16 models generate compliant code, with the leading model achieving 95%-100% CIS compliance, a fourfold improvement over humans at 23%-43%, with overall code quality improving by 19%-49%. The remaining 12 models fail not because they cannot generate code but because they cannot follow instructions with multiple constraints. For capable models, the approach requires no retraining and can be adopted through system prompts.

</details>


### [218] [ARISMA: Guidelines for AI- and LLM-Assisted Systematic Reviews, Scoping Reviews, and Mapping Studies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25050)
*Mahyar Tourchi Moghaddam,Mina Alipour*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出ARISMA标准，为系统综述中AI辅助方法的使用提供端到端的报告与集成规范，强调AI作为可检查、可基准测试、可记录和可逆的辅助工具，而非自主审阅者，核心原则是所有关键科学决策必须保持人类可解释、可审计和可问责。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着系统综述工作量和复杂度不断增加，AI/ML/LLM在综述各环节的应用日益广泛，但现有标准（如PRISMA 2020、PRISMA-S等）缺乏对AI使用的方法学适当性、验证方式、人类主导决策范围和AI参与报告的具体操作规范，导致AI在综述实践中的应用缺乏统一、可审计的指导。

Method: 本文开发ARISMA（AI Reporting and Integration Standard for Systematic Methods and Analysis）框架，包含生命周期分类法、流程指南、跨综述流程的分步建议、治理与来源模型、工具支持框架、AI集成报告清单和验证矩阵，并通过结构化专家咨询进行迭代优化。框架同时涉及法律、隐私、基础设施和可持续性考量。

Result: 产出一个实用且可审计的负责任AI辅助证据合成指南框架ARISMA，明确了AI在整个综述流程中的使用边界、验证要求、人类主导决策保持范围和AI参与的审计报告机制。

Conclusion: ARISMA为系统综述中AI的负责任使用提供了可操作的标准框架，填补了现有PRISMA系列标准在AI集成指导方面的空白，使AI辅助的证据合成既能提升效率，又能保持对人类可审计性和科学严谨性的保障。

Abstract: Systematic reviews, scoping reviews, mapping studies, and related evidence syntheses are increasingly difficult to conduct with fully manual workflows as search volumes, update cycles, and synthesis requirements continue to expand. At the same time, artificial intelligence, machine learning, and large language models are rapidly entering review practice across query formulation, screening, extraction, categorization, appraisal support, and reporting. Yet the empirical evidence remains uneven, task-dependent, and insufficient to justify unconstrained automation. Existing standards such as PRISMA 2020, PRISMA-S, PRISMA-ScR, PRISMA-P, PRESS, and SWiM remain essential, but none provides an end-to-end operational standard for when AI use is methodologically appropriate, how it should be validated, which review decisions must remain human-led, and how AI involvement should be reported so that readers can audit it. This paper proposes ARISMA, an AI Reporting and Integration standard for Systematic Methods and Analysis. ARISMA treats AI as an inspected, benchmarked, logged, and reversible assistant rather than an autonomous reviewer. It is built around one governing principle: every consequential scientific decision must remain human-interpretable, human-auditable, and human-accountable. The paper contributes a lifecycle taxonomy, process guidance, stepwise recommendations across the review pipeline, a governance and provenance model, a tool-support framework, an AI-integrated reporting checklist, and a validation matrix. It also addresses legal, privacy, infrastructure, and sustainability considerations. The framework was iteratively refined through structured expert consultation. The result is a practical and auditable guideline for responsible AI-assisted evidence synthesis.

</details>


### [219] [Model-Based Agentic Software Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25174)
*James C. Davis,Kelechi Kalu,Huiyun Peng,Parth V. Patil*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出模型驱动的智能体软件工程（MAGE）框架，通过外部化工程表示、约束行动、独立评估和保留人类权威，将商品化智能转化为可治理的工程环境，以解决编码智能体无法自动明确项目意图、系统结构和验收证据的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码智能体虽然提升了实现能力，但无法自动明确项目意图、系统结构和验收证据。当实现变得充裕而工程判断稀缺时，工作重心转向选择有用抽象、生产证据和确定验收义务，现有方法（更大提示、检索、逐变更审查）仍需智能体和工程师反复重构关键属性，缺乏系统性方案。

Method: MAGE框架包含两个核心问题处理：表示问题——外部化回答工程问题所需的最小目的性表示；权威问题——通过约束、传感器、验证器和门控赋予已沉淀义务成比例的权威。框架保持不确定意图的开放性，将反复重构和判断转化为可继承的耐久工程结构。该方法基于一个纵向案例开发，并经六个独立工业案例报告进行完善。

Result: 基于纵向案例和六个独立工业账户的验证，MAGE展示了外部化知识、受限行动、独立评估和保留人类权威如何组合成受治理的工程环境，并提出了判断该环境何时将商品化智能转化为持久工程进展的检验标准。

Conclusion: MAGE为构建可信自动化提供了一个框架和理论，主张通过外部化表示和分层权威治理，使智能体能力从'能实现'走向'能负责任地实现'，将稀缺的工程判断转化为可继承的结构性资产。

Abstract: Coding agents increase implementation capacity without automatically making project intent, system structure, or acceptance evidence explicit. As implementation becomes abundant relative to engineering judgment, the scarce work shifts toward choosing useful abstractions, producing evidence, and determining which obligations govern acceptance. Existing workflows address parts of this gap through larger prompts, repository retrieval, or perchange review, but still require agents and engineers to reconstruct consequential properties.
  As an alternative, we present Model-Based Agentic Software Engineering (MAGE). MAGE is a framework and a theory for building trustworthy autonomy from commodity intelligence. MAGE addresses a representation problem and an authority problem: it externalizes the smallest purposeful representation needed to answer an engineering question, then gives settled obligations proportionate authority through constraints, sensors, validators, and gates. It keeps uncertain intent open and turns recurring reconstruction and judgment into durable engineering structure that later work can inherit. We developed MAGE from a longitudinal case and refined it through six independently reported industrial accounts. Across these sources, MAGE explains how externalized knowledge, bounded action, independent evaluation, and retained human authority can compose into a governed engineering environment, and proposes tests of when that environment turns commodity intelligence into durable engineering progress.

</details>


### [220] [SPECMINE: A Large-Scale Corpus of Spec-Driven Development Artifacts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25202)
*Shyam Agarwal,Bogdan Vasilescu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: SPECMINE是一个大规模语料库，通过两项普查（broad census和Kiro census）捕获了GitHub公开仓库中的规范驱动开发（SDD）实践，涵盖超过47万个规范文件，并为11种工具收集了近6000个涉及规范的拉取请求及其变更集，以及超过240万个类型化引用，用于研究AI智能体时代软件规范如何转化为代码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自2025年以来，规范驱动开发（SDD）工具大量涌现，但这些工具产出的规范工件从未被大规模系统研究过，缺乏一个能够捕捉SDD实践全貌的语料库。

Method: 构建SPECMINE语料库，通过两项普查（broad census覆盖47万+个规范文件的广泛普查，以及针对Kiro工具特定布局的专项普查），并为每个规范补充仓库元数据、完整提交历史和解析后的文档结构；同时为11种工具收集涉及规范的拉取请求及其变更集，并建立包含242万个类型化引用的索引来建立规范与代码之间的独立链接。

Result: SPECMINE包含470,795个规范文件（跨越73,030个仓库，归属于17种工具），Kiro普查包含98,574个文件（12,910个仓库），以及11种工具下581个仓库中的5,992个涉及规范的拉取请求，外加2,421,323个类型化引用索引。

Conclusion: SPECMINE首次使社区能够在AI智能体时代大规模研究软件规范如何被编写、如何转化为代码，以及规范驱动的软件开发实践的具体模式。

Abstract: Spec-Driven Development (SDD) is a fast-emerging practice in which a structured natural-language specification, written by a developer, or (more often) drafted by an AI tool and then curated by the developer, drives an AI coding agent's implementation. A wave of tooling (GitHub Spec Kit [3], OpenSpec [4], AWS Kiro [5], and dozens of others) has appeared since 2025, yet the artifacts these tools produce have never been studied at scale. We present SPECMINE, a corpus that captures SDD in public GitHub repositories through two censuses: a broad census of spec.md/specs.md files covering most tools (470,795 files across 73,030 repositories, attributed to 17 named tools), and a Kiro census of its distinct requirements/design/tasks layout (98,574 files across 12,910 repositories). Each spec is enriched with full repository metadata, complete commit history, and parsed document structure. How a spec becomes code is itself an open question, so for 11 tools we sweep every pull request that touches a spec in their repositories with at least ten stars, capturing 5,992 such PRs across 581 repositories with their changesets. That makes the simplest workflow, spec and implementation changing together in one PR, directly observable, and a census-wide index of 2,421,323 typed references (1.28M to code files, 863k to sibling documents, 152k to PRs, 62k refs, 43k branches, 22k issues) gives a second, independent link from spec to code. SPECMINE lets the community study, for the first time, how software is specified in the age of AI agents.

</details>


### [221] [A Few Pages of Markdown: Committed AI Configuration and Lower Quality Cost after Coding-Agent Adoption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25241)
*Yegor Denisov-Blanch,Shyam Agarwal,Pavel Azaletskiy,Hao He,Rylan Schaeffer,Brando Miranda,Bogdan Vasilescu,Sanmi Koyejo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出RAMP（仓库AI成熟度画像），一个基于版本控制工件（如行为规则、编码标准、命名代理定义、多代理编排）的四级累积成熟度模型。在441个仓库中验证了其累积性、可复现性（97%的人类标注一致性），并发现AI配置工件具有"一次提交、永不修改"的特性（73.8%）。重新估计现有代理采用面板数据后发现，代理在各级成熟度下均加速开发（提交量增加28-38%），但质量分化显著：未提交AI配置的代理优先仓库的认知复杂度增幅（+53% vs +27%）和静态分析警告增幅（1.7倍）均更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究仅报告代理采用的平均效应，掩盖了团队之间的巨大差异。作者希望提供一个可复用的工具来刻画团队如何配置AI工具，并揭示成熟度与开发产出、代码质量之间的关联。

Method: 提出RAMP四级累积成熟度模型（行为规则/编码标准 → 命名代理定义 → 多代理编排），基于版本控制工件进行分类；在441个仓库上验证模型的累积性和人类标注可复现性；并按成熟度分层重新估计现有代理采用面板数据，比较不同层级的开发速度与代码质量指标。

Result: RAMP四级模型具有累积量表特性，独立标注在留出样本上的标签复现率达97%；73.8%的AI配置工件提交后从未修改。代理使各级成熟度仓库的提交量增加28-38%，但未提交AI配置的代理优先仓库认知复杂度增幅约为已配置仓库的2倍（+53% vs +27%），静态分析警告增幅为1.7倍。

Conclusion: 代理确实加速开发，但AI配置成熟度与代码质量呈正相关；由于成熟度是观察性变量，工程纪律或模型能力等混杂因素可能解释部分差距，因此将发现定位为假设生成性的，并发布RAMP作为可复用研究工具。

Abstract: Coding agents increase development velocity but also technical debt. Prior work reports only average effects across adopters, hiding wide differences between teams. We introduce RAMP (Repository AI Maturity Profile), a four-level cumulative maturity model grounded in version-controlled artifacts that teams commit to configure AI tools. RAMP runs from behavioral rules and coding standards through named agent definitions to multi-agent orchestration, with observed practice concentrated in the first three levels. Across 441 repositories the levels behave as a cumulative scale, and independent human annotation reproduces RAMP's repository-level labels on 97% of a held-out sample. Adoption is cumulative, forward-only, and set-and-forget: 73.8% of artifacts are committed once and never modified. Re-estimating an existing agent-adoption panel within each stratum, agents accelerate development regardless of maturity (28-38% more commits), but quality diverges: among agent-first repositories, where the contrast is identified, those without committed AI configuration show roughly twice the increase in cognitive complexity (+53% versus +27%) and 1.7x the increase in static-analysis warnings. Because maturity is observational, correlated engineering discipline or model capability may explain part of the gap; we present these findings as hypothesis-generating and release RAMP as a reusable instrument.

</details>


### [222] [Metis: Typed Runtime Mediation for Tool-Using Software Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25322)
*Jun Yu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Metis是一个多提供商运行时，将提供商流转换为类型化事件，在外部效应前显式化权限决策、干扰类别、终端结果和生命周期转换，通过30组真实I/O对和十案例故障矩阵验证了其分派、权限路由、子权限边界等能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件代理将概率模型输出连接到改变仓库、进程、网络和图形应用的操作上，需要一种运行时机制来显式且可检查地管理权限决策和生命周期转换，确保外部副作用的安全边界。

Method: Metis采用多提供商运行时架构，将提供商流转换为类型化事件，在执行路径上显式化权限决策、干扰类、终端结果和生命周期状态转换；通过30组匹配的真实I/O对进行四类调解，使用十案例故障矩阵和子边界消融实验评估其机制。

Result: 四类调解将中位耗时从25.958ms降至14.146ms，平均配对差异-12.295ms（95% bootstrap区间[-12.968,-11.694]），所有配对均更快；完整门控加注册表条件阻止了未授权效应并隐藏全部五个逃逸工具；决策仅权限预言机匹配全部十个声明案例。

Conclusion: 结果支持关于分派、权限路由、子权限和提供商有效迹闭合的有界声明，但不建立模型能力、语义安全性、回滚能力或对其它运行时的优越性。

Abstract: Software agents connect probabilistic model output to operations that change repositories, processes, networks, and graphical applications. We present Metis, a multi-provider runtime that converts provider streams into typed events before admitted calls reach external effects. Its execution path makes permission decisions, interference classes, terminal results, and lifecycle transitions explicit and inspectable. We evaluate these mechanisms on frozen source artifacts. Across 30 matched real-I/O pairs, four-class mediation reduced median elapsed time from 25.958 ms under forced serialization to 14.146 ms. The mean paired difference was -12.295 ms (95% bootstrap interval [-12.968, -11.694]), with mediation faster in all pairs. A ten-case fault matrix exposed duplicate-identifier and rollback limits. In a child-boundary ablation, the full gate-plus-registry condition blocked the declared unauthorized effect and hid all five escape tools. Removing both protections reversed both observations. A decision-only permission oracle matched all ten declared cases across five invocation routes. Five model conditions also completed a fixed Read-marker protocol in 3/3 trials each. These results support bounded claims about dispatch, permission routing, child authority, and provider-valid trace closure. They do not establish model competence, semantic safety, rollback, or superiority over another runtime.

</details>


### [223] [Point-in-Time Audit Before Alpha: Public-Archive Availability and a Negative Matched-Budget Study on BTC Perpetual Futures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25348)
*Baocheng Zeng,Jinhao Yang,Peilin Han,Kangnan He*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对Binance BTCUSDT USD-M永续合约的公开加密货币数据进行审计，发现无缺口五分钟要求未能满足（最长未修复交集304.58天），修订后保留727个完整UTC日并划分436/145/146天的训练/验证/历史留出集。审计器在冻结模板上实现40/40违规检测和0/40误拒，将空信号路径的假通过率从0.2910降至0.0625，但自适应代理未优于随机搜索，历史留出下所有方法IC为正但净Sharpe为负，最终报告了范围化的负面结果而非盈利声明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公开加密货币档案看似可用，但因子研究需要每个决策时点可观测且可执行的完整数据。现有数据可用性缺乏系统性审计，导致研究可能基于事后不可获得的"幻觉数据"，需要建立严谨的审计框架以区分建议与确定性审计、评估和留出访问。

Method: 使用事件时间、发布时间和可用时间三个维度审计Binance BTCUSDT USD-M永续合约公开数据。提出gapless五分钟要求（涵盖trade、mark、index、open interest四个流），失败后修订为以trade、mark、index和realized funding为核心流（open interest因发布时间未验证改为可选）。构建80个冻结的已知规则模板验证审计器，并比较完全审计与未审计条件下的空信号路径假通过率，最后在历史留出集上评估自适应代理。

Result: 初始gapless五分钟要求失败，最长未修复交集304.58天。修订后保留727个完整UTC日，划分436/145/146天数据。审计器在80个冻结模板上检测40/40违规并拒绝0/40合法模板。完全审计将空信号路径平均假通过从0.2910降至0.0625。在匹配的有效候选预算下，审计后的自适应代理与随机搜索持平，未建立优越性。历史留出中所有运行IC为正但净Sharpe为负。

Conclusion: 研究得出范围化的负面结果：在严格的数据可用性审计条件下，该加密货币因子研究未能证明盈利能力或代理优越性。这强调了数据完整性审计在金融研究中的关键作用，并提示公开数据档案的"表面可用"可能掩盖严重的时点可用性问题。

Abstract: Public cryptocurrency archives may appear usable when files exist, although factor research requires observations available and executable at each decision time. We audit public Binance BTCUSDT USD-M perpetual-futures data using event, publication, and availability times and separate proposal from deterministic auditing, evaluation, and holdout access. An initial gapless five-minute requirement for trade, mark, index, and open interest failed: the longest unrepaired intersection was 304.5729166666667 days. A disclosed revision made trade, mark, index, and realized funding the core streams and made open interest optional because its publication time was unverified. The revised mask retained 727 complete UTC days and supported a 436/145/146-day train, validation, and historical-holdout split. On 80 frozen known-rule templates, the auditor detected 40/40 violations and rejected 0/40 legal templates. Across ten null-signal paths, full auditing reduced mean false passes from 0.2910 to 0.0625. Under matched valid-candidate budgets, the audited adaptive agent tied random search and did not establish superiority. In the one-time historical holdout, all evaluated runs had positive IC but negative net Sharpe under primary costs. We therefore report a scoped negative result rather than a profitability or agent-superiority claim.

</details>


### [224] [Retry Amplification in Distributed Systems: A Systematic Analysis of Retry Policies and Their Role in Cascading Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25403)
*Rishabh Mehan,Jasmit Kaur Saluja*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究了分布式系统中重试机制的集体行为，提出了重试放大因子（RAF）这一量化指标，通过对200个开源Python微服务项目的分析发现重试逻辑的普遍存在及常见缺陷（如无退避、无随机化），并提出了自适应重试预算（ARB）方法，证明预算受限的重试能在保持接近无重试基线的成功率的同时恢复瞬时故障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重试机制是弹性分布式系统的标准组件，但每个调用点独立配置重试策略时，整个调用路径上各层同时重试所产生的集体行为（如请求风暴放大）远未被充分理解。现有指南多针对单一客户端，缺乏对系统级重试行为的分析与度量。

Method: 本文提出重试放大因子（RAF）度量指标；对200个开源Python微服务项目进行静态分析检测显式重试逻辑，并审计自身的误报率；通过模拟实验（每策略100次试验）评估不同重试策略在相关故障下的表现；归纳五种重试反模式，提出自适应重试预算（ARB）方法。

Result: 检测到11.5%的项目包含显式重试逻辑（审计后真实流行率约为41%）；被发现的项目中60.9%至少有一个无退避的配置，113个生产配置中仅1个随机化了延迟。模拟显示，在相关故障下，朴素标准重试策略将成功率从55.4%降至41.5%（低于不重试）；而预算约束的重试可维持接近无重试基线的成功率同时恢复瞬时故障。

Conclusion: 重试行为应作为系统级属性来设计，而非在每个调用点局部配置。预算受限的重试策略能够在性能和故障恢复之间取得平衡。

Abstract: Retry mechanisms are a standard component of resilient distributed systems, but their collective behavior, when every tier in a call path retries concurrently, is less well understood than the per-client guidance that produced them. This paper introduces the retry amplification factor (RAF), a metric quantifying the additional request volume that retry policies generate during partial failures. In a study of 200 open-source Python microservice projects, explicit retry logic is detected in 11.5%, and an audit of our own false negatives places true prevalence near 41%. Among the projects detected, 60.9% contain at least one configuration without backoff, and after manual verification exactly one of 113 production configurations randomizes its delay. We then evaluate these policies in simulation (n = 100 trials per strategy). Under correlated failures, a naive standard retry policy reduces the success rate from 55.4% to 41.5% relative to performing no retries at all. We catalog five recurring anti-patterns, propose Adaptive Retry Budgeting (ARB), and show that budget-constrained retries maintain success rates close to the no-retry baseline while still recovering from transient faults. These results indicate that retry behavior should be designed as a system-level property rather than configured locally at each call site.

</details>


### [225] [RotDroid: Cross-Orientation State Equivalence Testing for Detecting GUI Rotation Bugs in Android Apps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25425)
*Mengdi Qin,Bo Jiang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: RotDroid是一个用于检测Android应用屏幕旋转引发的GUI缺陷的测试框架，通过构建跨方向的语义等价GUI状态，结合微调的视觉语言模型RotVL进行等价性检查，在真实应用中发现94个此前未知的缺陷，其中47个已获开发者确认或修复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 屏幕旋转是Android应用中的基本交互，但常引发非崩溃性功能缺陷（如布局不一致、状态丢失），而现有方法缺乏有效的测试预言（oracle）来检查横竖屏间状态等价性。

Method: 1) 提出RotDroid测试框架，通过生成和变异状态保持动作序列（SPS）来构造跨方向的语义等价GUI状态；2) 构建RotBench数据集，包含配对的横竖屏GUI状态对；3) 开发RotVL，一个针对等价性检查进行微调的视觉语言模型，作为可靠的测试预言。

Result: 在合成和真实数据集上，RotVL优于现有最先进模型；在同等预算下RotDroid检测到的旋转缺陷多于现有技术；在大型开源和闭源应用研究中报告94个未知缺陷，其中47个已被开发者确认或修复。

Conclusion: RotDroid结合SPS构造技术与微调视觉语言模型作为测试预言，能有效检测Android应用旋转引发的GUI缺陷，并在实际应用验证中展现出显著的实践价值。

Abstract: Screen rotation is a fundamental interaction in Android applications, but it often introduces non-crashing functional failures (NCFs), such as layout inconsistencies and state loss, which are difficult to detect automatically. A key challenge is the lack of effective test oracles for checking cross-orientation state equivalence between portrait and landscape views. We propose RotDroid, a testing framework for detecting GUI rotation bugs via cross-orientation state equivalence. RotDroid generates and mutates State-Preserving action Sequences (SPS) to construct semantically equivalent GUI states across orientations. To support reliable oracle checking, we build RotBench, a dataset of paired portrait-landscape GUI states, and develop RotVL, a vision-language model fine-tuned for equivalence checking. Experiments on both synthetic and real-world datasets show that RotVL outperforms state-of-the-art models, and RotDroid detects more rotation-induced failures than existing techniques under equal budgets. In large-scale studies on open- and closed-source apps, RotDroid reports 94 previously unknown bugs, with 47 confirmed or fixed by developers, demonstrating its practical effectiveness.

</details>


### [226] [A Hybrid Usability Approach for Rating Evaluation of M-Commerce Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25550)
*Ahmad Ibtisam,Bilal Khan,Arshad Ali*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究聚焦于识别影响移动商务应用评级的可用性因素，构建了包含8个因素的混合可用性模型，并通过40名用户对5款知名M-commerce应用进行数据采集，使用前向逐步多元线性回归进行评级预测，最后用PRED(x)和K-fold技术验证模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动应用的成功依赖其有用性，而评级是衡量这一点的关键指标。现有可用性模型缺乏对M-commerce应用评级预测的系统性评估，因此需要探索并构建一个能够有效预测评级综合可用性模型。

Method: 首先调研现有可用性模型及标准，选取5款移动商务应用进行评估；构建包含学习性、一致性、人为因素、沟通性、有效性、可操作性、效率、满意度8个因素的混合可用性模型；从每个应用的40名用户收集数据；采用前向逐步多元线性回归分析各因素标准，提出评级预测模型；使用PRED(x)和K-fold技术进行模型评估与验证。

Result: 提出了基于前向逐步多元线性回归的M-commerce应用评级预测模型，并通过PRED(x)和K-fold技术对模型进行了评估和验证，确认了模型中显著影响评级的关键可用性因素。

Conclusion: 该混合可用性模型能够有效预测M-commerce应用的评级，为提高移动商务应用的可用性设计和评分表现提供了有价值的参考框架。

Abstract: The success of any mobile application relies on its usefulness and rating is considered as an important measure in this regard. This research work focuses on identifying usability factors, which contribute significantly towards the rating of M-commerce apps. This work intends to explore existing usability models consisting of different factors along with a set of criteria and evaluate in terms of rating estimation by considering 5 well-known mobile applications, namely (i) daraz, (ii) shophive, (iii) home shopping, (iv) Symbios. (v) yayvo. Then, this work provides a hybrid usability model for rating prediction of M-commerce applications. The initial hybrid usability model comprises of (i) learnability, (ii) consistency, (iii) human factors,(iv)communicativeness,(v)effectiveness, (vi) Operability, (vii) efficiency, (viii) satisfaction. Each factor consists of some criteria. Keeping in view the factors of hybrid usability model, the data was collected from 40 users for each application. Furthermore, Forward Stepwise Multiple Linear Regression based rating prediction model is suggested by analyzing each criterion of all factors of hybrid usability model. Finally, the model is assessed and validated by using PRED(x) and K-fold techniques.

</details>


### [227] [From General Agents to RCA Experts: A Self-Evolving Harness for Root Cause Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25661)
*Haiyu Huang,Jiewei Lyu,Zhihan Jiang,Jinyang Liu,Xiao He,Tieying Zhang,Wu Xiang,Michael R. Lyu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出OpsHarness，一种自进化的根因分析（RCA）框架。研究发现，利用现成通用代理（如Codex）配合外部自适应层（harness）的效果已超越从零构建专用RCA代理，但仍有差距。OpsHarness通过将诊断经验转化为可复用知识，实现自进化能力，在公共基准和工业部署中达到59.0%的top-1准确率，相比裸通用代理提升63.4%，相比基线RCA代理提升4.02倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM-based RCA主要有两条路径：直接使用通用代理（如Codex、Claude Code）或从零构建专用RCA代理。量化研究发现前者通常优于后者，但其准确率仍达不到生产要求，差距主要来自通用能力之外的"外部适配层"（harness）。因此作者认为应聚焦于构建自进化的外部harness，而非重新构建代理。

Method: OpsHarness包含数据平面和控制平面。数据平面结合分层运维知识与idea-card工具库；控制平面协调设置、诊断、进化和验证四个环节。在进化过程中，OpsHarness对比成功与失败的轨迹，将证据转化为原子化提案，并通过双门控验证流程防止过拟合和回归。

Result: 在两个公共基准和一个工业部署中，OpsHarness达到59.0%的top-1准确率，比裸通用代理提升63.4%，比基线RCA代理提升4.02倍。

Conclusion: LLM-based RCA应重利用现代强通用代理的能力，将工作重心放在外部harness上，特别是自进化能力——通过积累系统特定经验，让系统在持续使用中不断变好，从而逼近生产需求。

Abstract: Automated root cause analysis (RCA) with large language models (LLMs) has drawn growing attention. Today, SREs typically automate RCA with LLMs in one of two ways: directly using a general-purpose agent (e.g., Codex or Claude Code) for diagnosis, or building a specialized RCA agent from scratch. As mainstream general agents grow more capable and iterate quickly, our quantitative study finds that the former now often surpasses the latter. Its accuracy, however, still falls short of production needs, and this gap stems mainly from the external adaptation layer outside the agent's general capabilities, namely the harness. We therefore argue that LLM-based RCA should focus on this external harness, reusing the strong general capabilities of a modern agent rather than rebuilding an agent from scratch. A key capability of such a harness is to self-evolve, accumulating system-specific experience from past diagnoses so that it gets better the more it is used. We introduce OpsHarness, a self-evolving RCA harness that turns diagnosis experience into reusable expertise. Its data plane combines layered operational knowledge with an idea-card tool library, while its control plane coordinates setup, diagnosis, evolution, and verification. During evolution, OpsHarness contrasts successful and failed trajectories, converts their evidence into atomic proposals, and admits updates only through a dual-gate verification process designed to prevent overfitting and regression. Across two public benchmarks and an industrial deployment, OpsHarness achieves 59.0\% top-1 accuracy, improving over a bare general agent by 63.4\% and over baseline RCA agents by 4.02$\times$.

</details>


### [228] [Predicting Struggling Students in CS1 Programming Using Keystroke-Level Editing Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25769)
*Yasuyo Kofune,Kazumasa Shimari,Kenichi Matsumoto*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究利用按键级日志在CS1编程练习中早期检测陷入困境的学生，通过CodeBench平台数据对比三类特征配置，发现结合按键级与执行日志的特征在最早阶段预测效果最佳（AUROC 0.674），证明早期行为信号能有效预测学生最终能否解决问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部分学生在CS1编程练习中无法在练习结束前达到正确解答，等到成绩或最终结果显现问题时，教师提供及时干预帮助其恢复的机会可能已错过。因此需要在练习过程中尽早识别此类学生。

Method: 使用CodeBench平台的按键级实时代码编辑日志、执行日志和提交日志数据，将学生分为BT组（最终提交满分）和FS组（所有提交均为0分），比较三种特征配置（ExecOnly、CMOnly、Combined），在不同提交阶段评估预测FS学生的准确性。

Result: 在最早阶段，CMOnly优于ExecOnly（AUROC 0.654 vs 0.575），Combined比ExecOnly提升+0.098（AUROC 0.674）。所有配置中最早阶段预测信号最强。

Conclusion: 练习开始时的行为信号包含学生能否最终解决问题的有用线索，按键级编辑日志在早期优先排序方面比仅用执行日志提供了额外价值。

Abstract: This paper investigates the feasibility of early detection of struggling students during CS1 programming exercises using keystroke-level logs. Some students fail to reach a correct solution before the exercise ends, and by the time this becomes apparent from grades or final outcomes, the opportunity for timely instructor support aimed at helping them recover may have passed. We use data from the CodeBench platform, which records real-time code editing events at the keystroke level, alongside execution and submission logs. We define two outcome groups: Breakthrough (BT) students, whose prior submissions all receive 0% and whose final submission achieves full credit, and Fully Stuck (FS) students, whose submissions all receive 0% without reaching a correct solution. To examine this feasibility, we focus on two questions: (RQ1) whether adding keystroke-level editing features improves the prediction of FS students over execution-log features alone, and (RQ2) at which stage BT and FS students can be predicted most accurately. Experiments on the 2019-1 semester of the CodeBench dataset, comprising 507 students, compare three feature configurations: execution-based features (ExecOnly), CodeMirror-based features (CMOnly), and their combination (Combined). We evaluate prediction across successive submission-based stages during each exercise. In the earliest stage, CMOnly outperforms ExecOnly (AUROC 0.654 vs. 0.575), and Combined further improves over ExecOnly by +0.098 (AUROC 0.674). Across all configurations, the earliest stage yielded the strongest predictive signal. These findings indicate that behavioral signals present at the very start of an exercise contain useful clues about whether a student will ultimately solve the problem, and that keystroke-level editing logs provide additional value for early prioritization beyond execution logs alone.

</details>


### [229] [Beyond the Editing Canvas: Evidence Divergence in OOXML-to-LLM Ingestion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25880)
*Side Liu,Jiangpeng Liu,Jinwen Xin,Guojun Peng,Jiang Ming*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文发现OOXML文档在Microsoft Office与LLM提取流水线之间会产生"证据分叉"（evidence forks）——同一个规范合法的OOXML文件在不同消费者眼中呈现不同的证据视图，导致LLM可能消费到Office画布中不显示的内容。作者系统挖掘规范确认了21种证据分叉机制，测试结果显示4个原生LLM API在48-76%的试验中暴露了这些隐藏事实，并发现暴露程度主要由上游提取路径决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM流水线越来越多地将Word/Excel/PowerPoint等OOXML文件作为一等证据用于金融、合规和检索增强工作流，隐含假设是模型消费的证据与Microsoft Office编辑画布中显示的内容一致。作者质疑这一语义完整性假设，认为规范有效的同一OOXML文件可以产生不同的证据视图。

Method: 系统遍历和挖掘OOXML规范，确认了跨越Excel、Word和PowerPoint六个视图构建设维度的21种证据分叉机制；构建包含13个提取工具的提取面板和4个原生LLM API及7个web聊天机器人，每个测试文档包含一个"陷阱"事实（提取可见但在Office中不显示）；对16个流行开源LLM项目进行源代码级调查。

Result: 21种证据分叉全部被提取面板覆盖，13个工具均至少暴露一个分叉；4个LLM API在48-76%的试验中返回陷阱事实；11个接口中至少有一个在20/21机制中返回陷阱；暴露由上游摄取路径和提取器配置决定；开源项目默认摄取路径集中在受影响提取器家族。

Conclusion: OOXML到LLM流水线存在"多重真值"（plural ground truth）问题，语义完整性假设不可靠。证据分叉系统性存在且广泛影响工具和模型，且问题根源在模型上游的摄取路径。本文呼吁OOXML摄取契约需要明确说明哪些视图和语义角色会成为模型证据，并保留证据的推导来源。

Abstract: LLM pipelines increasingly ingest Office Open XML (OOXML) documents (Word, Excel, and PowerPoint files) as first-class evidence in financial, compliance, and retrieval-augmented workflows, implicitly assuming semantic integrity: that the evidence consumed by the model matches the content shown in the Microsoft Office suite editing canvas. We show that this assumption can fail in OOXML-to-LLM pipelines. The same specification-valid OOXML file can yield one evidentiary view in Microsoft Office and another when extracted for an LLM. Each view is treated as authoritative by its consumer, a condition we call plural ground truth. The ingestion contract rarely states which view and semantic roles become model evidence or preserves how that evidence was derived. We call the specification-grounded OOXML constructions that induce such divergence evidence forks.
  We systematically traverse and mine the OOXML specification and confirm 21 evidence forks across Excel, Word, and PowerPoint, spanning six dimensions of view construction. All 13 tools in our extraction panel emit evidence from at least one fork. We test four native-ingestion LLM APIs and seven web chatbots. Each test document carries a trap: a task-relevant fact exposed by extraction but not shown in Office. Across this 21-mechanism evaluation, the four APIs return the trap in 48--76% of trials. For 20 of 21 mechanisms, at least one of the eleven interfaces returns the trap. Our measurements further show that exposure is shaped upstream of the model by the ingestion path and extractor configuration. A source-level survey of sixteen popular open-source LLM projects further shows that default OOXML ingestion paths concentrate on affected extractor families.

</details>


### [230] [Answer Is Cheap, Show Me the Evidence! Augmenting Automated Vulnerability Assessment with Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25905)
*Shengyi Pan,Zelong Zheng,Jiayuan Zhou,Xing Hu,Xin Xia,Shanping Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出EAVA框架，利用大型语言模型（LLMs）进行软件漏洞（SV）评估并提供支持性证据。EAVA通过专业化LLM智能体处理富文本内容和项目信息，采用两阶段训练流程（先进行监督指令微调注入领域知识，再通过强化学习提升推理能力），并检索相似历史漏洞作为补充证据。实验表明EAVA在多个指标上优于最强基线5.3%至35.2%，消融实验和用户研究验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动化软件漏洞评估方法往往忽略富文本信息（如截图和代码片段）以及易受攻击项目的上下文信息，且只关注预测准确率而不提供解释或支持性证据，限制了分析人员在实际中验证不完美预测的实用性。因此需要一种既能提高评估准确性又能提供可解释证据的方法。

Method: EAVA框架的核心方法包括：1）使用专门的LLM智能体处理软件漏洞报告中的富文本内容和项目信息；2）构建两阶段训练流程——首先通过监督指令微调在自动标注的推理轨迹上注入领域知识，然后应用强化学习改进内在推理能力；3）检索相似历史漏洞作为补充证据来支持评估。

Result: 在新收集的软件漏洞报告数据集上，EAVA在多个指标上比最强基线方法提升了5.3%到35.2%。消融研究证实了评估特定模型训练和信息增强的有效性。与安全专家进行的用户研究进一步表明，EAVA提供的证据对实际软件漏洞评估是有用且实用的。

Conclusion: EAVA通过结合LLM智能体、两阶段训练和相似漏洞检索，有效解决了软件漏洞评估中信息利用不充分和缺乏可解释性的问题，在实际应用中具有较高的实用价值。

Abstract: Software vulnerability (SV) assessment helps prioritize remediation by characterizing reported vulnerabilities. Existing
  automated methods predict assessment results from SV reports (SVRs), but often overlook information in rich text, such as
  screenshots and code snippets, as well as contextual information about vulnerable projects. They also focus on prediction
  accuracy without providing explanations or supporting evidence, limiting their practical use when analysts must validate
  imperfect predictions. We propose EAVA, a framework that uses large language models (LLMs) to assess SVs and provide
  supporting evidence. EAVA employs specialized LLM agents to process rich-text content and project information, and builds a
  dedicated assessment model through a two-stage training pipeline. It first uses supervised instruction tuning on automatically
  annotated reasoning trajectories to inject domain knowledge, and then applies reinforcement learning to improve intrinsic
  reasoning. EAVA also retrieves similar historical vulnerabilities as supplementary evidence. Experiments on a newly collected
  SVR dataset show that EAVA outperforms the strongest baseline by 5.3 to 35.2 percent across multiple metrics. Ablation studies
  confirm the effectiveness of assessment-specific model training and information enrichment. A user study with security experts
  further demonstrates that the evidence provided by EAVA is useful and practical for real-world SV assessment.

</details>


### [231] [XREPOTEST: Benchmarking Multilingual Repository-Level Unit Test Generation for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25939)
*Dung Le Quang,Dong Cao Van,Nam Le Hai,Linh Ngo Van,Anh M. T. Bui,Phuong T. Nguyen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出XREPOTEST，一个多语言仓库级别的单元测试生成基准，覆盖Rust、Go、Julia、PHP和Ruby五种语言，通过容器化执行框架和多种上下文增强策略（文件级、LSP和检索式）来评估14个前沿LLM，并提出新的调用率（IR）指标。实验发现独立测试生成与仓库级性能之间存在显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM自动化单元测试生成的评估大多基于独立测试场景和少量编程语言，导致实际应用能力被高估，缺乏在真实仓库约束下的多语言综合评估基准。

Method: 构建多语言仓库级基准XREPOTEST，覆盖五种未被充分研究的编程语言；采用容器化执行框架模拟真实仓库约束；设计了文件级、基于LSP和基于检索的多种上下文增强策略；提出调用率（Invocation Rate, IR）指标来衡量测试是否真正触达目标功能；评估14个SOTA LLM模型。

Result: 实验表明独立设置与仓库级性能之间存在显著差距，且更丰富的上下文与测试可靠性之间存在权衡关系。XREPOTEST可以作为具有挑战性的基准。

Conclusion: XREPOTEST为在现实软件环境中推进可扩展且稳健的单元测试生成提供了有价值且具有挑战性的评估基准，数据集和代码已公开。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have shown promise for automated unit test generation, but existing evaluations largely rely on standalone settings and a narrow set of programming languages, overestimating real-world readiness. We introduce XREPOTEST, a multilingual repository-level benchmark for unit test generation spanning five underexplored languages: Rust, Go, Julia, PHP, and Ruby. XREPOTEST evaluates tests under realistic repository constraints using a containerized execution framework and multiple context augmentation strategies, including file-level, LSP-based, and retrieval-based context. Beyond standard metrics such as test pass rate and coverage, we propose Invocation Rate (IR) to assess whether generated tests meaningfully exercise the intended functionality. Experiments with 14 state-of-the-art LLMs, including Claude 4.5, GPT-5.2, DeepSeek V4-Pro, and Qwen families, reveal a substantial gap between standalone and repository-level performance, as well as trade-offs between richer context and test reliability. Overall, XREPOTEST provides a challenging and informative benchmark to advance scalable and robust unit test generation in realistic software environments. The dataset and code are publicly available at: https://github.com/solis-team/XRepoTest

</details>


### [232] [Vulnerable Code Search: Transferable Attack for Code Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26031)
*Kaicheng Wang,Liyan Huang,Jesse Thomason,Weihang Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种与编程语言无关、可迁移的对抗攻击方法，通过扰动代码中的标识符（不改变功能）使无关代码与目标查询相似，从而显著降低代码检索性能，MRR最高下降77%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前神经代码语言模型驱动的代码检索工具容易受到针对非功能性文本元素的对抗攻击，这种脆弱性尚未得到充分研究，需要揭示这一安全隐患。

Method: 提出一种跨编程语言、可迁移的对抗攻击方法。该方法扰动代码片段中的标识符而不改变代码功能，使代码嵌入与目标查询人为对齐。攻击使用小型嵌入模型（如CodeT5+）计算，但能有效迁移到大型闭源嵌入模型（如Voyage-code-3）和LLM（如Gemini-3.1-Pro）。

Result: 该攻击可使任意无关代码片段与查询之间的相似度显著提升，导致SOTA模型的关键检索指标（如MRR）最多下降77%，且在小模型上计算的攻击可迁移到大型闭源模型。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明当前代码搜索方法存在严重脆弱性，需要开发更鲁棒、更具语义感知能力的代码检索方法。

Abstract: Reliable code retrieval is crucial for developer productivity and effective code reuse. However, current neural code language models (CLMs) powering search tools are susceptible to adversarial attacks targeting non-functional textual elements. In this paper, we introduce a programming language-agnostic, transferable, adversarial attack that exploits this CLM vulnerability. Our approach perturbs identifiers within a code snippet without altering the snippet's functionality to artificially align the code with a target query. We demonstrate that our attack, even when computed using smaller code embedding models, such as CodeT5+, is highly effective and transferable to larger, closed-source embedding models, like Voyage-code-3, or LLMs like Gemini-3.1-Pro. Our attack can increase the similarity between the query and arbitrary, irrelevant code snippets, consequently degrading key retrieval metrics such as the Mean Reciprocal Rank (MRR) of state-of-the-art models by up to 77%. The experimental results highlight the fragility of current code search methods and underscore the need for more robust, semantic-aware approaches.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [233] [ToolMinimize: Auditing and Rewriting LLM Agent Tool Calls to Minimize Privacy Exposure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24957)
*Wenbiao Li,Yuqiao Xu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一个名为 \system{} 的中间件，通过拦截和重写工具调用的参数，结合模式感知的必要性分析（含删除、泛化、替换、截断四种操作），最小化LLM代理向第三方服务暴露的隐私敏感数据。在三款生产LLM上的实测显示隐私成本降低81.2-92.0%，且保持100%任务有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM代理在工具调用参数中常规性地包含超出工具所需范围的隐私敏感数据（PSD），每一次调用都会跨越信任边界将数据暴露给第三方服务。现有防御手段（如允许/阻止门控、信息流控制）无法重写参数值，而PII检测工具无法识别隐含的PSD（如医院名称暗示诊断信息）。

Method: 提出 \system{} 中间件，拦截工具调用并重写参数至工具功能所需的最小数据量。方法包括：（1）模式感知的必要性分析，无需额外元数据即识别必需字段；（2）四种重写操作：删除、泛化、替换、截断；（3）可选的LLM内容必要性层，用于剥离自由文本字段中与任务无关的PSD。

Result: 在三款LLM（GPT-4o、Claude 3.5 Sonnet、Llama-3.3-70B）的307个工具调用上，隐私成本降低81.2-92.0%，参数级任务有效性100%（TOST等价p<0.001）。在25个未标注的MCP模式上将隐私成本降低79.0%。加入LLM内容必要性层后，隐私成本降低提升至85.1-95.6%。中位延迟仅1.77ms。

Conclusion: \system{} 证明通过参数级重写而非简单门控，可以在不牺牲任务有效性的前提下大幅降低LLM代理的隐私泄露风险，且开销极低，具有实际部署的可行性。

Abstract: LLM agents routinely include privacy-sensitive data (PSD) in tool call arguments beyond what the invoked tools require, crossing trust boundaries to third-party services on every invocation. A controlled measurement on three production LLMs (GPT-4o, Claude 3.5 Sonnet, Llama-3.3-70B) shows that 81--88\% of tool calls include unnecessary PSD under default prompts; explicit privacy instructions still leave 36--76\% over-sharing. Existing defenses gate calls (allow/block) or label flows (information-flow control) but cannot \emph{rewrite} argument values, and PII detection tools miss implicit PSD like ``Memorial Sloan Kettering'' (a hospital name that implies a diagnosis). We present \system{}, a middleware that intercepts tool calls and rewrites their arguments to the minimum data necessary for tool functionality, combining schema-aware necessity analysis with four operations: removal, generalization, substitution, and truncation. Live validation on 307 tool calls across the three LLMs above reduces privacy cost by 81.2--92.0\% at 100\% argument-level task validity (TOST equivalence $p{<}0.001$ at $Δ{=}1.0$); on 25 unannotated Model Context Protocol (MCP) schemas, by 79.0\% with no \texttt{minimum\_necessary} metadata. An optional LLM content-necessity layer strips task-irrelevant PSD from otherwise-necessary free-text fields, raising live-LLM reduction to 85.1--95.6\% and author-schema reduction from 71.1\% to 90.9\%. Median latency is 1.77\,ms.

</details>


### [234] [Retrieved But Not Reliable: A Survey on Attacks, and Defenses in Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24977)
*Minh Tran,Cuong Dang,Tuc Nguyen,Khanh-Tung Tran,Minh Huynh Nguyen,Trinh Chau,Kien Le,Do Xuan Long,Jiahao Zhang,Hoang D. Nguyen,Thanh Le,Suhang Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一份统一的、流水线感知的RAG鲁棒性综述，形式化了语料库、检索器和生成器上的威胁模型，将攻击分为准确性、隐私和公平性三大目标，并从检索、重排、生成和回溯四个阶段综述防御方法，同时总结了鲁棒性基准和可解释性方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检索增强生成（RAG）在提升大语言模型事实性和减少幻觉的同时，引入了新的鲁棒性和安全风险（如语料库投毒、后门攻击、隐私泄露和公平性违规）。现有综述对攻击者目标、威胁模型以及RAG全流水线各阶段的防御措施处理有限，缺乏统一视角。

Method: 采用统一且流水线感知的方法：形式化语料库、检索器和生成器上的威胁模型；按准确性、隐私和公平性三大目标组织攻击分类；从检索、重排、生成和回溯四个阶段综述防御策略；并补充鲁棒性基准和可解释性方法的总结。

Result: 提供了一份覆盖RAG全流水线的鲁棒性综述，系统性地分类了攻击目标与威胁模型，梳理了各阶段的防御手段，并整理了用于评估和解释RAG鲁棒性的基准与可解释性方法。

Conclusion: 该综述为RAG领域提供了统一的鲁棒性分析框架，填补了现有综述在攻击者目标、威胁模型和阶段化防御方面的空白，有助于更深入评估和解释RAG系统的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) enhances large language models by grounding outputs in external knowledge, improving factuality and reducing hallucinations. At the same time, the retrieval-augmented pipeline introduces new robustness and security risks, including corpus poisoning, backdoor attacks, privacy leakage, and fairness violations. Despite rapid progress in this area, existing surveys remain limited in their treatment of attacker objectives, threat models, and stage-specific defenses across the full RAG pipeline. This survey presents a unified and pipeline-aware overview of RAG robustness. We formalize threat models over the corpus, retriever, and generator, and organize attacks into three main objectives: accuracy, privacy, and fairness. We further review defenses from a pipeline-aware perspective, covering the retrieval, rerank, generation, and traceback stages. In addition, we summarize robustness benchmarks and explainability methods for more deeply evaluating and explaining RAG robustness.

</details>


### [235] [Static Detection of Post-Quantum Cryptographic Algorithms in Stripped Binaries for Digital Forensic Examination and Migration Assurance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25122)
*Muhammad Shaheer Bin Junaid*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Kestrel，一种静态分析方法，用于在剥离后的二进制代码中识别标准化的格基密码方案ML-KEM和ML-DSA。Kestrel通过检测数论变换（NTT）常数表来识别这些算法，在128个测试样本中实现零误报的理想召回率，并在生产系统上发现了12个未编目的包含ML-KEM的程序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前缺乏从编译后的二进制代码中验证量子脆弱算法是否已被获批的后量子算法替代的方法。现有的密码发现工具依赖符号、库依赖和运行时行为来识别算法，但这些信号在二进制被剥离、静态链接和优化后全部消失，导致无法验证后量子密码迁移的实际落实情况。

Method: Kestrel通过检测构成算法算术运算所依赖的只读数据——数论变换（NTT）常数表来识别ML-KEM和ML-DSA。它从公开方案参数中提取指纹，并通过归一化和多重集匹配过程进行定位，同时从解析上建立误报概率。

Result: 在四个独立实现谱系及所有构建变换（包括编译器级混淆）上的实验中，Kestrel在128个样本中实现了128个的召回率，且零误报。应用于生产Linux系统上的6,224个二进制文件时，发现12个未编目程序包含ML-KEM，包括OpenSSH密钥交换程序和容器管理栈。Kestrel还成功区分了真实的后量子实现与仅宣称而无实际代码支持的情况，并在取证实验中从未分配空间恢复了已删除二进制文件中的检测。

Conclusion: Kestrel为密码迁移保证、软件供应链检查和后量子取证检查提供了实用的基础。研究还揭示了一个重要发现：在一些项目中，后量子代码是通过语言运行时进入生产环境的，而发布这些项目的组织本身并未意识到这一点。

Abstract: Currently, there is no method to verify from compiled binary code whether a quantum-vulnerable algorithm has been replaced by an approved post-quantum algorithm. Cryptographic discovery tools identify algorithms by symbols, library dependencies, and runtime behaviour; however, all these signals are destroyed by stripping, statically linking, and optimising a binary. This paper presents Kestrel, a static analysis method for identifying the standardised lattice-based schemes ML-KEM and ML-DSA in stripped binary code. Kestrel identifies ML-KEM and ML-DSA by detecting the number-theoretic transform constant tables that form the read-only data upon which the arithmetic depends. The fingerprints Kestrel derives from public scheme parameters are localised by means of a normalisation-and-multiset-matching procedure; the false-positive probability is established analytically. In experiments on four independent implementation lineages and all build transformations, including compiler-level obfuscation, Kestrel achieved recall of 128 of 128 with zero false positives. Applying Kestrel to 6,224 binaries on a production Linux system disclosed twelve uncatalogued programs containing ML-KEM; these included the OpenSSH key-exchange program and the container-management stack. In several of these programs, post-quantum code entered production through the language runtime without the awareness of the projects distributing them. Kestrel distinguishes genuine post-quantum implementations from advertised claims not backed by the underlying code, attributes each detection to its originating codebase, and, in a forensic disk-image trial, recovered a detection from unallocated space after the deleted binary could no longer be reconstructed. Thus, Kestrel provides a practical basis for cryptographic migration assurance, software supply-chain inspection, and post-quantum forensic examination.

</details>


### [236] [CA-less Mutual Co-Signing of Documents over a Unidirectional Visual Channel with Transported Hardware Attestation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25144)
*Dmytro Diikun*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出并分析了一种无需证书权威和中间服务器的双移动设备文档互签协议，该协议仅通过单向、有损、低带宽的光学信道（屏幕动画代码配合摄像头）通信，利用硬件安全元素（Secure Enclave/StrongBox）提供密钥信任根，并通过两级哈希锚定解决交互式联合签名的循环依赖问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统双设备联合签名通常依赖证书权威或中间服务器作为信任路径，且需要双向可靠的通信信道。在仅有单向、有损、低带宽光学信道且无第三方信任设施的场景下，如何实现安全且可验证的双方互签是一个尚未解决的挑战。

Method: 核心技术贡献是一个两级哈希锚定机制：第一方在不知道第二方身份的情况下先承诺文档，第二方身份随后被绑定到该承诺而不使第一方签名失效。密钥信任基于平台安全元素生成的可信硬件认证令牌，通过喷泉码在全视觉信道上传输，并加密绑定到联合签名中。该协议在iOS/Android上用ECDSA P-256（Secure Enclave/StrongBox）、SHA-256、Apple App Attest/Play Integrity和LT喷泉码实现。

Result: 给出了完整的威胁模型，定义了四个安全属性（锚定绑定、联合签名不可分离性、认证绑定的密钥来源、签名后篡改证据），并将其归约到标准假设（哈希碰撞抵抗和签名方案EUF-CMA安全），安全元素建模为理想签名预言机。实现了跨iOS/Android的工作原型，并配有独立第三方离线验证器。

Conclusion: 该协议证明了在无证书权威、无中间服务器、仅依赖单向有损视觉信道的条件下，仍可实现具有可证明安全性的双设备文档联合签名，为离线或受限环境下的协同签名提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: We describe and analyze a protocol for mutual co-signing of a document by two mobile devices that (i) communicate only over a one-way, lossy, low-bandwidth optical channel (an animated on-screen code read by the counterparty's camera), (ii) use no intermediary server on the trust path, and (iii) use no certificate authority. Trust in each party's public key is instead grounded in a hardware attestation token produced by the platform secure element, transported in full over the visual channel by a rateless (fountain) code and cryptographically bound into the co-signature. The core technical contribution is a two-stage hash anchor that removes the circular signing dependency inherent to interactive co-signing: the first party commits to the document before the identity of the second party is known, and the second party's identity is later bound to that commitment without invalidating the first signature. We give a threat model, define four security properties (anchor binding, co-signature inseparability, attestation-bound key provenance, and post-signing tamper evidence) and reduce them to standard assumptions (collision resistance of H and EUF-CMA security of the underlying signature scheme), with the secure element modeled as an ideal signing oracle. We report a working instantiation on iOS/Android using ECDSA P-256 in the Secure Enclave/StrongBox, SHA-256, Apple App Attest / Play Integrity, and an LT-style fountain code, together with an independent third-party verifier that recomputes all anchors and checks both signatures fully offline.

</details>


### [237] [MACGen: Toward Functionally Correct and Secure Code Generation via Multi-Agent Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25457)
*Miseon Yu,Jaehoon Choi,Younghan Lee,Yunheung Paek*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出MACGen，一个多智能体框架，通过将规划、安全分析、代码合成和审查分离为独立角色，仅传递结构化产物而非完整对话历史，在CWEval和BaxBench基准上将安全且正确的代码生成率分别平均提升19.61和10.57个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型生成的代码经常包含安全漏洞，因为安全代码生成需要同时优化功能正确性和安全性这两个多目标问题。现有方法要么依赖外部安全知识注入（但通用建议难以转化为任务特定实现），要么依赖多智能体对话反馈（但共享对话会导致角色边界模糊和上下文膨胀）。

Method: MACGen采用多智能体框架，包含四个角色：规划器（制定满足功能需求的分步计划）、安全顾问（识别可能的CWE并生成任务特定安全指南）、编码器（基于计划和安全指南生成代码）、审查员（提供分视角的反馈）。关键设计是智能体之间不共享完整对话历史，而是仅传递上游阶段的结构化产物，以强化角色专业化并控制上下文增长。

Result: 在CWEval和BaxBench基准上，MACGen相比直接提示（direct prompting）在F&S@1指标上分别平均提升19.61和10.57个百分点。

Conclusion: MACGen通过结构化的多智能体协作和定向信息传递机制，有效解决了安全代码生成中功能正确性与安全性难以兼顾的问题，验证了角色分离和受限信息流在复杂代码生成任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Despite their strong ability to generate code, large language models often fail to produce secure code, as their outputs frequently contain security vulnerabilities. Secure code generation is inherently challenging because it requires solving a multi-objective problem: functional correctness and security. Existing approaches address this challenge by injecting external security knowledge or by using agentic feedback and iterative refinement. However, guideline retrieval often leaves the generator to translate generic advice into task-specific secure implementations, while shared-dialogue multi-agent feedback can blur role boundaries and suffer from context bloat.
  We present MACGen, a multi-agent framework that integrates planning, security analysis, code synthesis and refinement to jointly optimize security and functionality. A planner constructs a step-by-step plan to satisfy functional requirements. A security advisor identifies likely CWEs and synthesizes task-specific guidelines, a coder then generates code grounded in these artifacts, and a reviewer issues perspective-separated feedback. Rather than sharing full dialogue histories, each agent receives only structured artifacts from upstream stages, enforcing role specialization and reducing uncontrolled context growth.
